- **bauplan-data-assessment**: Assesses whether a business question can be answered with data available in a Bauplan lakehouse. Maps business concepts to tables and columns, checks data quality on the relevant subset, validates semantic fit, and renders a verdict: answerable, partially answerable, or not answerable. Produces a structured feasibility report. Use when a user brings a business question, asks 'can we answer this', wants to know if the data supports an analysis, or before building a one-off analysis or pipeline. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-data-assessment) · ★16)

- **bauplan-data-quality-checks**: Generates data quality check code for bauplan pipelines and ingestion workflows. Invoked by the bauplan-data-pipeline and bauplan-safe-ingestion skills, or directly by the user. Produces expectations.py for pipelines or validation logic for WAP scripts. Output is always code, never reports. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-data-quality-checks) · ★16)
- **bauplan-data-semantics**: Reads and drafts the semantic layer of a Bauplan lakehouse — the table-level and column-level documentation carried in Iceberg as `comment` and field `doc`. Use to find out what a column means before querying it, to diagnose a query that returned a plausible but wrong number, or to draft documentation for columns whose meaning is not obvious from name and type. Also invoked by bauplan-data-pipeline and bauplan-migrate-to-typed-sdk when a pipeline needs documentation written. Read-only: drafts documentation text, never edits model code or runs pipelines. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-data-semantics) · ★16)
- **bauplan-debug-and-fix-pipeline**: Diagnose a failed Bauplan job, pin the exact data state, collect evidence, apply a minimal fix, and rerun. Evidence first, changes second. - **bauplan-explore-data**: Explores data in a Bauplan lakehouse safely using the Bauplan Python SDK. ([BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills](https://github.com/BauplanLabs/bauplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/bauplan/skills/bauplan-explore-data) · ★16)

- **bb-invoke-conformal**: 调用 Cadence Conformal LEC (`lec`) 做逻辑等价性检查（RTL↔netlist 或 netlist↔netlist），替代开源流程缺失的 LEC 角色（netgen 只做 SPICE-LVS）。Workflow: (1) render do-file; (2) run lec -LPGXL -TCLmode -NOGui -Dofile; (3) parse Equivalent / NON-EQUIVALENT。触发场景：(1) bba-guru-synthesis post-synth 验证 RTL↔综合网表等价；(2) bba-guru-pd post-route 验证综合网表↔post-route 网表等价；(3) ECO 后证明仅改了那一处；(4) 显式 /bb-invoke-conformal。 ([essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets](https://github.com/essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bb-invoke-conformal))
- **bb-invoke-genus**: Cadence Genus 25.12 逻辑综合 → ASAP7 NLDM 门级网表（多 V_T、PLE）。Stage-0 candidate-first：写 run-local logic candidate（不覆盖 canonical），可验证 strategy levers（dont_use/clock-gating，retiming 默认禁用），native 单位归一化（ps→ns），native power report 指针。Workflow: (1) render TCL (--candidate-dir + strategy); (2) run genus -files; (3) parse (--time-unit 1ps --build-id --candidate-netlist-sha)。触发：(1) bba-guru-synthesis 需生成 netlist；(2) PD 反馈 area/path 超标重综合；(3) 显式 /bb-invoke-genus。 ([essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets](https://github.com/essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bb-invoke-genus))
- **bb-invoke-innovus**: 调用 Cadence Innovus 25.12 执行统一 Place&Route（init/floorplan/place/cts/route/opt/extract/streamout/verify_drc），取代开源 Magic+QRouter+KLayout 三件套；内建 NanoRoute + ccopt + Quantus extractRC，三角 MMMC（ss/tt/ff），产出 saveDesign .enc + routed.def + GDSII + DRC/timing 报告。Workflow: (1) render TCL; (2) run innovus -files; (3) parse DRC/timing/area。触发场景：(1) bba-guru-pd 在综合网表上跑 P&R；(2) DRC/timing fail 后迭代；(3) 显式 /bb-invoke-innovus。 ([essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets](https://github.com/essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bb-invoke-innovus))
- **bb-invoke-jaspergold**: 调用 Cadence JasperGold 2026.03 做形式验证：SVA 性质证明（prove/falsify/unknown）、CDC 跨时钟域违例扫描、RTL lint。开源 Babel 完全没有形式验证能力（bb-check-cdc 只是 pyverilog AST 启发式）。Workflow: (1) render SGL/TCL; (2) run jaspergold; (3) parse proven/falsified/unknown + CDC violations。触发场景：(1) bba-guru-verification 跑 SVA 性质证明；(2) bb-check-cdc 用 JasperGold CDC 引擎替代 AST 启发式；(3) RTL lint；(4) 显式 /bb-invoke-jaspergold。 ([essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets](https://github.com/essiloan/gpgpu-chiplets/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bb-invoke-jaspergold))
